// FUNC 180021fc0 __security_check_cookie
void __cdecl _security_check_cookie(uintptr_t StackCookie)
{
  __int64 v1; // rcx

  if ( StackCookie != _security_cookie )
ReportFailure:
    _report_gsfailure(StackCookie);
  v1 = __ROL8__(StackCookie, 16);
  if ( (_WORD)v1 != 0 )
  {
    StackCookie = __ROR8__(v1, 16);
    goto ReportFailure;
  }
}

unsigned __int64 __fastcall _alloca_probe()

    do
    {
      StackLimit -= 4096;
      *StackLimit = 0;
    }
    while ( v1 != StackLimit );

}

// FUNC 180022040 __raise_securityfailure
BOOL __fastcall _raise_securityfailure(struct _EXCEPTION_POINTERS *ExceptionInfo)
{
  HANDLE CurrentProcess; // rax

  SetUnhandledExceptionFilter(nullptr);
  UnhandledExceptionFilter(ExceptionInfo);
  CurrentProcess = GetCurrentProcess();
  return TerminateProcess(CurrentProcess, 0xC0000409);
}

// FUNC 180022074 __report_gsfailure
void __cdecl __noreturn _report_gsfailure(uintptr_t StackCookie)
{

  uintptr_t v2; // [rsp+40h] [rbp+8h] BYREF

  v2 = StackCookie;

    __fastfail(2u);
  capture_previous_context(&GS_ContextRecord);
  GS_ContextRecord.Rip = retaddr;
  GS_ContextRecord.Rsp = (DWORD64)&v2;
  qword_180037360 = retaddr;
  GS_ContextRecord.Rcx = v2;
  GS_ExceptionRecord = -1073740791;
  dword_180037354 = 1;
  dword_180037368 = 1;
  unk_180037370 = 2;
  _raise_securityfailure((struct _EXCEPTION_POINTERS *)&GS_ExceptionPointers);
}
